Small n objective:
```
def _obj(x):
    x = np.maximum(x,0) # SLSQP constraints handle but numerical
    s=x.sum()
    if s <= 0: return 1e9
    y=x/s
    b=np.convolve(y,y)
    return float(b.max())
```
But SLSQP may go outside; objective nonneg. Constraint sum1. We can use parameter x length n-1 with last=1-sum x to avoid equality constraint; bounds [0,1] for first n-1 and sum≤1. Pseudo:
```
def _score_quick(a):
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <0.01: return inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    return 2*len(a)*b.max()/(s*s)
```
This can be used for candidates.

Small DE details:
```
def _softmax_p(q_full, ref=0.0): # q_full length n-1
    vals = np.concatenate((q_full, [ref]))
    m = np.max(vals)
    e = np.exp(vals-m)
    return e/e.sum()
def _score_from_p(p):
    b = np.convolve(p,p)
    return 2*len(p)*b.max()
```
Since sum=1, score=2nM. For DE objective maybe return M not score to reduce scaling? But comparing score. `obj(q)=_score_from_p(softmax)`; returns score. It is smooth plus max. DE minimization over q with bounds. Use `tol=1e-12`, `maxiter` based on n and time. For n=3, q dim2.

def _exact_score(arr):
   try: return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr]))
   except: return inf

import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len

def _to_arr(x): ...
def _normalized(a): a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); return a/s if s>0 else None
def _conv_self(a): c=np.convolve(a,a); return c # direct
# for speed maybe FFT:
def _conv_fft(a):
    fsize=next_fast_len(2*len(a)-1)
    F=rfft(a,fsize)
    c=irfft(F*F,fsize)[:2*len(a)-1]
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0; c[~finite]=0; return c
# direct for n≤512 probably fine; use direct to avoid FFT imaginary tiny? direct exact.
def _score_from_b(a,b): s=a.sum(); if s<0.01: inf; return 2*len(a)*b.max()/(s*s)
def _fast_score(a): b=_conv_self(a); return ...
```

LP line search:
```
def _line_search(p, g, Mcurrent, max_t=2.0):
    best_t=0; best_m=Mcurrent
    ts = [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.5,2.0]
    for t in ts:
       if t>max_t: break
       arr = p + t*(g-p)
       if np.min(arr) < -1e-12: continue
       arr = np.maximum(arr,0)
       arr /= arr.sum() # if t changes? sum exactly 1 if g sum1, but round
       b=conv(arr,arr); m=b.max()
       if m < best_m*(1-1e-12): update
    if best_t>0: refine: lo=max(0,best_t*0.5), hi=min(max_t,best_t*1.5 or best_t+0.2); golden 8
    return best_t, best_m, best_arr
```

LP solve wrapper:
```
def _solve_LP(p, T, n):
    A=_build_A(p)
    c=-np.ones(n)
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, T), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
    if res.success and res.x is not None:
       g=np.maximum(res.x,0)
       s=g.sum()
       if s>1e-12: return g/s
    return None
```
If T<=0 return None.

But `linprog` with many variables bounds list length 192 okay. Could be slow if called hundreds. We can use method='highs'. If not available? scipy>=1.6 likely. Could fallback to 'simplex'. Wrap exceptions.

Improve function:
```
def _improve(p, seed, time_limit):
    p = normalized(p)
    best=normalized(p); best_score=fast_score
    state vars: no_improve=0, shrink=0.03
    while time < deadline:
       # compute b(current)
       b=conv(current); M=...
       # maybe if M < bestM update
       # try LP with T = M*(1-shrink) (clamp min 0.5*M)
       T = max(0.5*M, M*(1-shrink))
       g=solve_LP(p,T)
       if g is None:
          # gradient direction or perturb
          d = -_subgrad(p,b,active set using softmax weights beta=1000)
          project, line search
       else:
          # line search including t=1, maybe t up to 2
          t,tM,arr=line_search
          if t>0 and tM < M_current:
             p=arr; shrink=max(0.01, shrink*0.97) ; no_improve=0
          else:
             # try shrink=0? or gradient
             no_improve +=1
             if no_improve==5: shrink=max(0.01, shrink*0.5)
             if no_improve>20:
                p = normalize(p + rng.normal(0, noise, n)); noise maybe 0.1*sqrt(M)?
                # or p = 0.5p+0.5*best
                no_improve=0
                shrink=0.05
       # update best if currentScore < bestScore
       # if bestScore below target maybe break
    return best
```

Pseudo full:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog=None

def _clean_parent():
    try:
       h=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if h is None: return None
       a=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).ravel()
    except: return None
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0, posinf=0, neginf=0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    if a.sum() <=0: return None
    return a

def _normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return None
   return a/s
```

Fast score:
```
def _score_arr(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    mb=b.max()
    return float(2*a.size*mb/(s*s))
```
Need if b has tiny negative? conv nonnegative but maybe due float. use max(0,b). max.

Exact score call:
```
def _exact_score(a):
    try:
       vals=[float(x) for x in np.clip(a,0,1000)]
       return float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
    except Exception:
       return math.inf
```

Resample:
```
def _resample(a,n):
   if a.size == n: return a.copy()
   if a.size ==1: return np.full(n,a[0])
   if n < a.size:
       # average blocks of size a.size/n? Use linear indices then cluster?
       x_old=np.arange(a.size); x_new=np.round(np.linspace(0,a.size-1,n)).astype(int)
       # if duplicate indices, later average? simple nearest then sum normalize.
       return a[x_new]
   else:
       x_old=np.linspace(0,a.size-1,a.size)
       x_new=np.linspace(0,a.size-1,n)
       return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```
Normalize after.

Initial heuristics for n:
```
def _init_heuristics(n, parent, rng, count=8):
    i=np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1)
    out=[]
    def add(arr):
       arr=np.asarray(arr,float)
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
       if arr.sum()>1e-12:
           out.append(arr/arr.sum())
    if parent is not None:
       add(_resample(parent,n))
    add(np.ones(n))
    # asymmetric polynomials
    for a,b in [(1,0),(2,0),(0.5,0),(0,1),(0,2),(1,1),(2,1),(5,0.5)]:
       arr=(i+1e-3)**a * (1.0001 - i)**b # avoid zero endpoints
       add(arr)
    # exponentials
    for lam in [0.5,1,2,5,10]:
       add(np.exp(-lam*i)); add(np.exp(-lam*(1-i)))
    # sine/cos powers
    for q in [1,2,4]: add(np.abs(np.sin(np.pi*i))**q)
       add((1+np.sin(np.pi*i))**q)
       add((1+np.cos(2*np.pi*i))**q) # may zeros
    # Gaussian bumps not centered
    for c in [0.25,0.5,0.75]: for w in [0.1,0.25,0.5]: add(np.exp(-((i-c)/w)**2))
    # random dirichlet
    for alpha in [0.1,0.5,2,10]: add(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n,alpha))) maybe too large; for n large, okay.
    # parent perturbations
    if parent is not None:
       p0=_normalize(_resample(parent,n))
       if p0 is not None:
          for s in [0.05,0.1,0.2]: add(p0*(1+s*(rng.standard_normal(n)/np.linalg.norm(...))) )
    # Return unique top maybe 20 by fast score
    score sorted
```
Need avoid too many; top 10.

Improve function with LP. Need precompute info for n:
```
def _lp_info(n):
    m=2*n-1
    info=[]
    for k in range(m):
       l0 = 0 if k < n else k-(n-1)
       l1 = k if k < n else n-1
       j0 = k - l1
       j1 = k - l0
       info.append((k,j0,j1,l0,l1))
    return info

def _build_A(p, info, A=None):
    n=len(p); m=2*n-1
    if A is None: A=np.empty((m,n))
    else: A.fill(0)
    for k,js,je,l0,l1 in info:
        A[k, js:je] = p[l1:l0-1:-1]
    return A
```
Check slice when l0=0,l1=0: p[0:-1:-1] okay. When l1>l0 okay. For k=0 js=0 je=1.

Objective function for `_solve_lp`:
```
def _solve_lp(p, T, info, A_buf, b_buf):
    if T <= 0: return None
    A=_build_A(p,info,A_buf)
    b_buf.fill(T)
    if linprog is None: return None
    try:
       res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b_buf, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
       if res.success and res.x is not None:
          g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
          g=np.where(g>0,g,0)
          s=g.sum()
          if s>1e-12:
             return g/s
    except Exception:
       pass
    return None
```
If method highs unavailable, try method=None. We can set `try methods=['highs','highs-ds','revised simplex']`? linprog methods: 'highs', 'highs-ds', 'revised simplex', 'simplex'. Use available. Options maybe not accepted for simplex. Simple: 
```
methods = ['highs', 'highs-ds'] if hasattr else ['simplex']
for method in methods: try ...
```

Line search function:
```
def _evaluate_dir(p, g, Mcur, max_t=3.0):
    # g normalized nonneg
    safe = max_t
    if max_t >1:
       diff = g - p
       pos = diff > 1e-15
       if pos.any():
          safe = min(safe, (p[pos] / diff[pos]).min() * 0.999)
          if not np.isfinite(safe) or safe <=0: safe=1.0
    ts = [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0]
    if safe>1.0: ts.append(1.2); ts.append(1.5); ts.append(2.0)
    best_t=0; best_m=Mcur; best_arr=p
    for t in ts:
       if t>safe: continue
       arr=p + t*(g-p)
       if t>1.0 and arr.min() < -1e-12: continue
       arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
       s=arr.sum()
       if s<=0: continue
       arr/=s # sum unchanged if g,p sum1; for t>1 sum still1? p + t(g-p) sum =1+t(1-1)=1; yes
       m=np.convolve(arr,arr).max()
       if m < best_m - 1e-15:
           best_m=m; best_t=t; best_arr=arr
    # optional local refinement if best_t>0
    if best_t>0:
       lo=max(0.0, best_t*0.5)
       hi=min(safe, max(best_t*1.5, best_t+0.1))
       # golden 6 iterations
       gr=(1+5**0.5)/2
       a=lo; b=hi
       c=b- (b-a)/gr; d=a+ (b-a)/gr
       # evaluate function manually with cache? Use max; 6*2 eval ok
       def f(t):
          arr=p + t*(g-p)
          if min(arr)<-1e-12: return 1e9
          arr=np.maximum(arr,0); arr/=arr.sum(); return np.convolve(arr,arr).max()
       # We need already eval best; just loop simple
       for _ in range(6):
          if f(c) < f(d): b=d; d=c; c=b-(b-a)/gr? standard
       # Simpler: add 10 points in [lo,hi]
       for t in np.linspace(lo,hi,11): ...
    return best_t,best_m,best_arr
```

   lo=max(0,best_t*0.5); hi=min(safe, best_t*1.5)
   if hi<=lo: hi=min(safe,best_t+0.2)
   for t in np.linspace(lo,hi,15): evaluate update

Gradient fallback direction:
```
def _gradient_direction(p,b,beta=500.0):
    mb=b.max()
    z=np.exp(beta*(b-mb))
    z/=z.sum()
    w=z
    grad=_gradient_weights(p,w)
    d=-grad
    d-=d.mean()
    # normalize by L1/n
    norm = np.linalg.norm(d,1)
    if norm <1e-12: return None
    d=d/norm
    # project step direction? We'll line search with`q = p + step*d` projected to simplex.
    return d
```
Line search for gradient with step sizes: Need maintain sum and nonneg. We can for each step `arr = p + step*d`; `arr = _project_simplex(arr)` (sum1). Steps based on `tau = [0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005, ...]`. Since d normalized L1, step in units of mass. For n=192, d components maybe O(1/n). L1 normalized to1; step 0.05 moves 5% mass. Use.
Project simplex:
```
def _project_simplex(v,target=1.0):
    n=v.size
    x=np.asarray(v,float)
    # project onto simplex sum target, nonnegative
    u=np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    ind=np.arange(1,n+1)
    cond=(u - cssv/ind) > 0
    if not cond.any():
       return np.full(n,target/n)
    i=int(np.max(np.where(cond)[0]))
    theta=(cssv[i] - target)/(i+1)
    y=np.maximum(x-theta,0)
    # adjust to exact target due numeric
    s=y.sum()
    if s <=0: return np.full(n,target/n)
    return y*(target/s)
```
Standard projection gives sum target but with target not 1; formula uses target? For simplex with sum target, projection onto {x≥0,sum=target} is max(x-theta,0) with theta solving sum=target. The standard for target 1 uses sorted u, cssv, cond. For target s, theta=(cssv[i]-s)/(i+1). Need adjust: y=max(x-theta,0); s=y.sum(); return y*target/s maybe fine.

Main loop:
```
deadline = start + time_limit
no_imp=0
shrink=0.05
noise=0.1
for it in range(max_iters):
   if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.1: break
   # current M
   b=np.convolve(p,p); M=b.max()
   if M < best_M*(1-1e-12): best_M=M; best_arr=p.copy(); no_imp=0
   else: no_imp += 1
   # if best_M lower than lower bound? break if 2*n*best_M <=1.500? no.
   T = M * (1 - shrink)
   if T < 0.5*M: T=0.5*M
   g = _solve_lp(p,T,info,A_buf,b_buf)
   moved=False
   if g is not None:
       # If g nearly zero? skip
       if np.linalg.norm(g-p,1) > 1e-5:
          t,tM,arr = _line_search(p,g,M)
          if t > 0 and tM < M*(1 - 1e-12):
              p = arr; moved=True
              no_imp=0
              shrink = max(0.01, shrink*0.97)
              noise = max(0.01, noise*0.95)
          else:
              # try t=1? line search included; if no, maybe accept g if score global? no
              no_imp +=1
              if no_imp >= 3:
                 shrink = max(0.01, shrink*0.5)
       else:
           no_imp +=2
   else:
       # gradient fallback
       d=_gradient_direction(p,b)
       if d is not None:
          for step in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]:
             arr=_project_simplex(p + step*d,1)
             m=np.convolve(arr,arr).max()
             if m < M*(1-1e-12):
                 p=arr; moved=True; no_imp=0; break
       if not moved:
          no_imp +=1
   if no_imp > 15:
       # perturb
       p = _normalize(p + noise*(rng.standard_normal(n)))
       no_imp=0
       shrink=min(0.2, shrink*1.5)
       # sometimes return to best
       if rng.random()<0.3:
           p = 0.5*best_arr + 0.5*p
   if best_M <= 0? break
return best_arr,best_M
```

`_project_simplex` target sum 1. Formula:
```
def _project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    n=v.size
    x=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(x)
    ind=np.arange(1,n+1, dtype=float)
    cond = (x - cssv/ind) > 0
    if not cond.any(): return np.full(n,target/n)
    i = int(np.argmax(cond)) # if using argmax on bool? np.argmax returns first True. But we want largest index where cond true; cond is true for i less than threshold? Standard: theta = (sum_{j=1}^i u_j -1)/i, choose largest i satisfying u_i - (sum-1)/i >0. cond array decreasing? Could use `i = np.where(cond)[0][-1]`.
    theta = (cssv[i] - target) / (i+1)
    y = np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
    s=y.sum()
    if s <=0: return np.full(n,target/n)
    return y * (target/s)
```
Yes.

`_resample` for n<a.size: if use rounding duplicates, sum may not be 1 after average; normalize. For parent shape, okay.

`_generate_inits` with many exponentials. Ensure no np overflow: (n+1)^a for a=5 with n=512 ~3.4e13 okay. exp(-lambda x) okay. For uniform add. Random Dirichlet with n large and alpha maybe 0.5 okay. We can limit random count to 3.

Potential candidate all zeros after clip? add checks sum.

Is each A column j covered for k from j to j+n-1? But there are zeros at positions outside slices. Need fill zeros each time. Could avoid by precomputing for each column the k slice and assign zeros once? p changes, but number of nonzeros total m*n/2? ~n^2/2. Fill zero O(n^2) okay. For n=512, 1M fill.

`linprog` method selection:
```
def _solve_lp(...):
   if linprog is None: return None
   c_v = _solve_lp.c_vec
   if _solve_lp.n != n: set
   methods = ('highs','highs-ds','revised simplex')
   last_exc=None
   for method in methods:
      try:
         res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method=method, options={'presolve':True})
         ...
      except Exception as e:
         last_exc=e
   return None
```
But `highs-ds` may not exist. `revised simplex` maybe slow; skip. Use `method='highs'`, fallback `method=None` (default). `method=None` chooses HiGHS. Good.

Line search function: Use `np.convolve(arr, arr).max()` direct. If arr sum 1, M maybe. For large n, conv returns full. Use `np.max`. Ensure no negative due rounding: max with 0.
```
def _M_of(a):
   c=np.convolve(a,a)
   m=c.max()
   return 0.0 if m<0 and m>-1e-15 else float(m)
```

In `_improve`, we call `_M_of` many times; could reuse conv of current p only when needed. For line search, arr new each.

Let's write `_improve` more concretely.
```
def _improve(n, p0, deadline, rng_seed):
    p=_normalize(p0)
    if p is None: p=np.ones(n)/n
    info = _lp_info(n)
    A_buf = np.empty((2*n-1,n))
    b_buf = np.empty(2*n-1)
    bounds = [(0,None)]*n
    best_arr = p.copy()
    b0 = np.convolve(p,p); best_M = float(max(0,b0.max()))
    no_imp = 0
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b = np.convolve(p,p); M = float(max(0,b.max()))
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M; best_arr = p.copy(); no_imp=0
        else:
            no_imp += 1
        it +=1
        # maybe reduce shrink
        if it < 10: shrink = 0.02
        elif no_imp < 5: shrink = 0.05
        elif no_imp < 15: shrink = 0.1
        else: shrink = 0.2
        T = M * (1.0 - shrink)
        if T > M - 1e-15: T = M
        if T <= 0: T = M
        g = _solve_lp(p, T, n, info, A_buf, b_buf, bounds)
        improved = False
        if g is not None:
            # protect if g sum small? normalized
            diff_norm = float(np.linalg.norm(g-p,1))
            if diff_norm > 1e-6:
                t,tM,arr = _line_search(p,g,M)
                if t > 1e-5 and tM < M * (1 - 1e-10):
                    p = arr
                    improved=True
                    no_imp=0
                    # maybe increase shrink slightly? keep
            else:
                no_imp += 2
        else:
            # try T=M once
            g = _solve_lp(p,M,n,info,A_buf,b_buf,bounds)
            if g is not None:
                t,tM,arr = _line_search(p,g,M)
                if t > 1e-5 and tM < M*(1-1e-10):
                   p=arr; improved=True; no_imp=0
            # gradient fallback
            if not improved:
                d=_gradient_direction(p,b)
                if d is not None:
                   for step in (0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
                       arr = _project_simplex(p + step*d, 1.0)
                       m = _M_of(arr)
                       if m < M*(1-1e-10):
                          p=arr; improved=True; no_imp=0; break
        if not improved:
            no_imp +=1
            if no_imp > 20:
                # perturb
                mode = rng.integers(3)
                if mode==0:
                    p = p + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
                elif mode==1:
                    idx = rng.choice(n, size=max(1,n//10), replace=False)
                    p[idx] *= rng.uniform(0.5,2.0, len(idx))
                else:
                    p = 0.7*p + 0.3*best_arr
                p = _normalize(p)
                if p is None: p=best_arr.copy()
                no_imp=0
        # if best_M reaches theoretical lower plus tiny? can break if 2n*best_M < 1.500? maybe not.
        if 2*n*best_M <= 1.500? Could be below theoretical? If achieved 1.499, good. Stop maybe if <=1.498. Use if <=1.498? But not know. Use <=1.500? If lower 1.5, cannot. Add: if best_M*2*n <= 1.5005: break? To save time. But if actual optimum <1.5, stopping early bad. Don't.
    return best_arr,best_M
```
Potential infinite loop if LP/g line search no improve because T=M direction p. Need perturb. Good.

Line search max_t: If t>1 and arr negative skip. Need compute safe exactly:
```
def _safe_max_t(p,g,max_candidate):
    safe=1.0
    if max_candidate>1.0:
       den=g-p
       mask=den>1e-14
       if mask.any():
          ts=(p[mask]/den[mask])
          safe=min(safe, np.min(ts)*(1-1e-12))
       safe=min(safe,max_candidate)
    return safe
```
If min ts huge, safe max_candidate. If safe <0.1? for t≤1 still ok because mask for >1 no effect; safe=1. For t list >1 only if safe>1.001. Good.

for a in cands:
   s=2*n*_M_of(a)
   if finite: scored.append((s,a))
scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
return [a for _,a in scored[:limit]]
```

Implement `_block_polish` carefully:
```
def _block_polish(p, deadline, rng, max_moves=5000):
    p=_normalize(p); n=len(p)
    b=np.convolve(p,p); best_M=float(max(0,b.max())); best=p.copy()
    widths=[3,5,7,10,15,25]
    widths=[w for w in widths if w< n]
    if not widths: widths=[max(2,n//2)]
    for it in range(max_moves):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       w = int(rng.choice(widths))
       maxlo = max(1, n-w)
       lo = int(rng.integers(0, maxlo+1))
       hi = min(n, lo+w)
       if hi-lo < 2: continue
       q = p[lo:hi]
       s_in = q.sum()
       if s_in <= 1e-15: continue
       q = q/s_in
       s_out = 1.0 - s_in
       # sample x
       xs = np.linspace(0,1,16)
       # local around s_in
       xs = np.concatenate([xs, np.clip(s_in + rng.normal(0,0.1,20),0,1)])
       best_x=s_in; best_local=best_M; best_arr_local=p
       base_out = p.copy(); base_out[lo:hi]=0.0
       if s_out <=0: outside zeros; but s_in~1 if block all mass, then outside zero; handle.
       for x in xs:
           arr = base_out * (0.0 if s_out<=0 else (1.0-x)/s_out)
           arr[lo:hi] = x*q
           # due x=0/1 exact
           if s_out>0:
               arr = base_out * ((1.0 - x)/s_out)
           else:
               arr = np.zeros(n)
               arr[lo:hi] = x*q  # if x=1 only
           if arr.sum() <=0: continue
           m = _M_of(arr)
           if m < best_local - 1e-15:
               best_local=m; best_x=x; best_arr_local=arr
       if best_local < best_M - 1e-14:
           p=best_arr_local; best_M=best_local; best=p.copy()
    return best,best_M
```
The base_out * scale per x O(n); sample 36; if 5000 moves, 5000*36*192 ~34M operations, okay maybe few seconds. For n=512, 100M, okay. Limit moves by time. Use `max_moves` dynamic.

But if `base_out` zeros and scale by 0, fine. If s_out very small, division by small; but s_in from p sum maybe near1, okay. Use if s_out <1e-12 treat outside zero, only x=1 relevant; skip.

for t in np.linspace(lo,hi,13): if t<=safe: eval
```
Maybe t=0.01 best, lo=0, hi=0.15; good.

`_gradient_direction`: Use `_weights_logsumexp` inline. Ensure exp no overflow: `beta*(b-Mb)` ≤0. If b-Mb negative large maybe underflow to0, fine. Sum >0. If not, uniform.
`d = -grad; d -= d.mean()`; `norm = np.linalg.norm(d, ord=1)`. if norm<1e-12 return None. return d/norm.
Potential: gradient components can be O(1) but norm L1 maybe O(1). step list.

```python
import math
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _linprog
except Exception:
    _linprog = None


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return a / s


def _M_of(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else math.inf
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    return 0.0 if m < 0.0 and m > -1e-15 else m


def _score_fast(a, n=None):
    if n is None:
        n = len(a)
    m = _M_of(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01 or not math.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _exact_score(a):
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)]
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
        return sc if math.isfinite(sc) else math.inf
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def _parent_array():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if h is None:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.sum() <= 1e-15:
        return None
    return a


def _resample(a, n):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if n < a.size:
        old = np.arange(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        new = np.linspace(0.0, a.size - 1.0, n)
        idx = np.rint(new).astype(int)
        idx = np.clip(idx, 0, a.size - 1)
        return a[idx]
    old = np.linspace(0.0, a.size - 1.0, a.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, a.size - 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, a)


def _project_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    x = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    ind = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (u - cssv / ind) > 0.0
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    i = int(np.where(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[i] - float(target)) / float(i + 1)
    y = np.maximum(x - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, target / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return y * (float(target) / s)


def _lp_info(n):
    info = []
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        l0 = 0 if k < n else k - (n - 1)
        l1 = k if k < n else n - 1
        j0 = k - l1
        j1 = k - l0
        info.append((k, j0, j1, l0, l1))
    return info


def _build_A(p, info, A=None):
    n = p.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if A is None:
        A = np.empty((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        A.fill(0.0)
    for k, j0, j1, l0, l1 in info:
        A[k, j0:j1 + 1] = p[l1:l0 - 1:-1]
    return A


def _solve_lp(p, T, info, A, b, bounds):
    if _linprog is None:
        return None
    if T <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(T):
        return None
    n = p.size
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    _build_A(p, info, A)
    b.fill(T)
    for method in ("highs", "highs-ds"):
        try:
            res = _linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method=method,
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
            if res.success and res.x is not None:
                g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                g = np.where(g > 0.0, g, 0.0)
                s = float(g.sum())
                if s > 1e-9:
                    return g / s
        except Exception:
            pass
    try:
        res = _linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds)
        if res.success and res.x is not None:
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            g = np.where(g > 0.0, g, 0.0)
            s = float(g.sum())
            if s > 1e-9:
                return g / s
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _gradient_direction(p, b):
    mb = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    beta = 1000.0
    z = np.exp(beta * (b - mb))
    z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
    zs = float(z.sum())
    if zs <= 1e-15 or not math.isfinite(zs):
        w = np.full(b.size, 1.0 / b.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = z / zs
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return None
    wr = w[::-1]
    conv_g = np.convolve(wr, p)
    grad = 2.0 * conv_g[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    d = -grad
    d -= d.mean()
    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(d, ord=1))
    if norm <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(norm):
        return None
    return d / norm


def _line_search(p, g, Mcur, max_t=3.0):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    safe = 1.0
    if max_t > 1.0:
        den = g - p
        mask = den > 1e-14
        if np.any(mask):
            ts = p[mask] / den[mask]
            ts = ts[np.isfinite(ts) & (ts > 0.0)]
            if ts.size:
                safe = min(safe, float(np.min(ts)) * (1.0 - 1e-12))
        safe = min(safe, float(max_t))

    best_t = 0.0
    best_m = float(Mcur)
    best_arr = p.copy()

    ts = [0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0]
    if safe > 1.02:
        for t in (1.15, 1.35, 1.65, 2.0, 2.6):
            if t < safe:
                ts.append(min(t, safe))
    ts.append(min(1.0, safe))

    for t in ts:
        if t <= 0.0 or t > safe + 1e-12:
            continue
        arr = p + t * (g - p)
        if t > 1.0 and float(np.min(arr)) < -1e-10:
            continue
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15 or not math.isfinite(s):
            continue
        arr = arr / s
        m = _M_of(arr)
        if m < best_m * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best_m = m
            best_t = t
            best_arr = arr

    if best_t > 0.0:
        lo = max(0.0, best_t * 0.45)
        hi = min(safe, max(best_t * 1.35, best_t + 0.08))
        if hi <= lo:
            hi = min(safe, best_t + 0.15)
        if hi > lo:
            for t in np.linspace(lo, hi, 15):
                if t <= 0.0 or t > safe + 1e-12:
                    continue
                arr = p + t * (g - p)
                if t > 1.0 and float(np.min(arr)) < -1e-10:
                    continue
                arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
                s = float(arr.sum())
                if s <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                arr = arr / s
                m = _M_of(arr)
                if m < best_m * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                    best_m = m
                    best_t = t
                    best_arr = arr

    return best_t, best_m, best_arr


def _improve(n, p0, deadline, rng):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    if p is None:
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size != n:
        p = _normalize(_resample(p, n))
        if p is None:
            p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    best_arr = p.copy()
    best_m = _M_of(p)

    if n < 2:
        return best_arr, best_m

    info = _lp_info(n)
    A = np.empty((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        cur_b = np.convolve(p, p)
        cur_m = float(np.max(cur_b)) if cur_b.size else math.inf
        if not math.isfinite(cur_m):
            p = best_arr.copy()
            cur_b = np.convolve(p, p)
            cur_m = float(np.max(cur_b)) if cur_b.size else math.inf

        if cur_m < best_m * (1.0 - 1e-13):
            best_m = cur_m
            best_arr = p.copy()

        improved = False

        if it < 8:
            shrink = 0.015
        elif stall < 5:
            shrink = 0.04
        elif stall < 12:
            shrink = 0.08
        elif stall < 20:
            shrink = 0.14
        else:
            shrink = 0.22

        T = cur_m * (1.0 - shrink)
        if T <= 0.5 * cur_m:
            T = 0.5 * cur_m
        if T > cur_m:
            T = cur_m

        g = _solve_lp(p, T, info, A, b, bounds)
        if g is None:
            g = _solve_lp(p, cur_m, info, A, b, bounds)

        if g is not None:
            if float(np.linalg.norm(g - p, ord=1)) > 1e-7:
                t, tm, arr = _line_search(p, g, cur_m)
                if t > 1e-6 and tm < cur_m * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                    p = arr
                    if tm < best_m * (1.0 - 1e-13):
                        best_m = tm
                        best_arr = arr.copy()
                    improved = True

        if not improved:
            d = _gradient_direction(p, cur_b)
            if d is not None:
                for step in (0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.0075):
                    q = _project_simplex(p + step * d, 1.0)
                    m = _M_of(q)
                    if m < cur_m * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                        p = q
                        if m < best_m * (1.0 - 1e-13):
                            best_m = m
                            best_arr = q.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break

        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 22 or (it > 60 and stall > 10):
                r = rng.random()
                if r < 0.4:
                    cand = np.maximum(best_arr, 0.0) + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                elif r < 0.7:
                    cand = p.copy()
                    k = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 8)))
                    idx = np.sort(rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False))
                    cand[idx] *= rng.uniform(0.45, 2.2, size=k)
                else:
                    cand = 0.75 * best_arr + 0.25 * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    cand += 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                cand = _normalize(cand)
                if cand is None:
                    cand = best_arr.copy()
                p = cand
                stall = 0
        else:
            stall = 0

    return best_arr, best_m


def _block_polish(p0, deadline, rng, max_moves=100000):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, math.inf
    n = p.size
    if n < 4:
        return p, _M_of(p)

    best_m = _M_of(p)
    best_arr = p.copy()
    widths = [3, 5, 7, 10, 15, 25, 40]
    widths = [w for w in widths if 2 <= w <= max(2, n // 3)]
    if not widths:
        widths = [max(2, min(n // 4, 10))]

    stall = 0
    for _ in range(max_moves):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        w = int(rng.choice(widths))
        maxlo = max(1, n - w)
        lo = int(rng.integers(0, maxlo + 1))
        hi = min(n, lo + w)
        if hi - lo < 2:
            continue

        qs = p[lo:hi]
        qsum = float(qs.sum())
        if qsum <= 1e-14:
            continue
        q = qs / qsum
        outsum = 1.0 - qsum
        if outsum <= 1e-12:
            outsum = 0.0

        xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 11)
        xs = np.concatenate(
            [xs, np.clip(qsum + rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, size=9), 0.0, 1.0)]
        )

        base_out = p.copy()
        base_out[lo:hi] = 0.0

        local_best_m = best_m
        local_best_arr = best_arr
        local_best = False

        for x in xs:
            x = float(x)
            if x < -1e-12 or x > 1.0 + 1e-12:
                continue
            x = min(1.0, max(0.0, x))
            if outsum > 0.0:
                arr = base_out * ((1.0 - x) / outsum)
            else:
                arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            arr[lo:hi] = x * q
            if float(arr.sum()) <= 1e-14:
                continue
            m = _M_of(arr)
            if m < local_best_m * (1.0 - 1e-13):
                local_best_m = m
                local_best_arr = arr.copy()
                local_best = True

        if local_best:
            p = local_best_arr
            if local_best_m < best_m * (1.0 - 1e-13):
                best_m = local_best_m
                best_arr = p.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 350:
                break

    return best_arr, best_m


def _generate_inits(n, parent, rng, limit=6):
    cands = []

    def add(a):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size != n:
            a = _resample(a, n)
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s > 1e-12 and math.isfinite(s):
            cands.append(a / s)

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(max(1, n))

    if parent is not None:
        pr = _normalize(_resample(parent, n))
        if pr is not None:
            add(pr)
            add(pr * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n)))
            add(pr * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, size=n)))
            add(pr * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=n)))

    add(np.ones(n))

    for a in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        add((x + 1e-5) ** a)
        add((1.0 - x + 1e-5) ** a)
        add((x + 1e-5) ** a * (1.0 - x + 1e-5) ** 2.0)
        add((x + 1e-5) ** 2.0 * (1.0 - x + 1e-5) ** a)

    add(1.0 / (x + 0.02))
    add(1.0 / (1.0 - x + 0.02))

    for k in (1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0):
        add(1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(k * (x - 0.4))))
        add(1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(k * (x - 0.6))))
        add(1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(k * (x - 0.5))))

    for c in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
        for w in (0.08, 0.18, 0.35):
            add(np.exp(-(((x - c) / w) ** 2)))
            add(np.exp(-(((x - (1.0 - c)) / w) ** 2)))

    add(np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * x)) ** 2.0)
    add(np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * x)) ** 4.0)
    add((1.0 + np.sin(np.pi * x)) ** 2.0)

    for alpha in (0.2, 1.0, 5.0):
        try:
            add(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha, dtype=np.float64)))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not cands:
        return [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n]

    scored = []
    for a in cands:
        sc = _score_fast(a, n)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, a))
    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
    seen = []
    out = []
    for _, a in scored:
        arr = a.copy()
        # de-duplicate by cheap fingerprint
        fp = tuple(np.round(arr * 1e4).astype(np.int64).tolist())
        if fp not in seen:
            seen.append(fp)
            out.append(arr)
            if len(out) >= limit:
                break
    if not out:
        out.append(scored[0][1].copy() if scored else np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - 60.0)
    if deadline > t0 + budget - 5.0:
        deadline = t0 + budget - 5.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent_raw = _parent_array()
    parent = _normalize(parent_raw)

    if parent is not None:
        primary_n = int(parent.size)
        default_n = primary_n
    else:
        primary_n = 192
        default_n = 192

    if parent is not None:
        best_arr = parent.copy()
    else:
        best_arr = np.full(default_n, 1.0 / default_n, dtype=np.float64)

    best_score = _exact_score(best_arr)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_arr = np.full(default_n, 1.0 / default_n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = _exact_score(best_arr)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = math.inf

    lengths = []

    def _add_len(L):
        try:
            L = int(L)
        except Exception:
            return
        if L < 4 or L > max(1024, primary_n):
            return
        if L not in lengths:
            lengths.append(L)

    _add_len(primary_n)
    _add_len(256)
    if budget >= 700.0:
        _add_len(384)
    if budget >= 1000.0:
        _add_len(512)
    if not lengths:
        lengths = [192, 256]

    # Primary length first, then useful resolutions.
    lengths = [primary_n] + [L for L in lengths if L != primary_n]

    tasks = []
    for L in lengths:
        is_primary = (L == primary_n)
        n_inits = 5 if is_primary else 2
        inits = _generate_inits(L, parent, rng, limit=n_inits)
        for j, init in enumerate(inits):
            w = (10 - j) if is_primary else (5 - j)
            if w < 1:
                w = 1
            tasks.append((L, init, float(w)))

    # Do not allow very tiny slices.
    min_remaining_for_task = 12.0

    rem_weight = 0.0
    for _, _, w in tasks:
        rem_weight += w

    seed_offset = int(seed + 101)
    task_idx = 0
    for L, init, w in list(tasks):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - min_remaining_for_task:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < min_remaining_for_task:
            break
        if rem_weight <= 1e-12:
            tmax = max(5.0, rem - 5.0)
        else:
            tmax = rem * (w / rem_weight)
            rem_weight -= w
        tmax = max(8.0, min(tmax, 420.0))
        if tmax > rem - 5.0:
            tmax = max(5.0, rem - 5.0)

        try:
            arr, m = _improve(L, init, time.monotonic() + tmax,
                              np.random.default_rng(seed_offset + task_idx * 91))
            arr = _normalize(arr)
            if arr is not None:
                sc = _score_fast(arr, L)
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    ex = _exact_score(arr)
                    if math.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = ex
                        best_arr = arr.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

        task_idx += 1

    # Final polish on the current best, if time remains.
    if best_arr is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
        t_pol = min(120.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0))
        if t_pol > 8.0:
            try:
                pol, pm = _block_polish(
                    best_arr,
                    time.monotonic() + t_pol,
                    np.random.default_rng(seed_offset + 777),
                )
                pol = _normalize(pol)
                if pol is not None:
                    sc = _score_fast(pol, len(pol))
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                        ex = _exact_score(pol)
                        if math.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best_score = ex
                            best_arr = pol.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final exact validation.
    try:
        final_score = _exact_score(best_arr)
    except Exception:
        final_score = math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(final_score):
        fb = np.full(default_n, 1.0 / default_n, dtype=np.float64)
        try:
            fs = _exact_score(fb)
            if math.isfinite(fs):
                return [float(x) for x in fb]
        except Exception:
            pass
        return [1.0]

    out = []
    for v in best_arr:
        vv = float(v)
        if not math.isfinite(vv):
            vv = 0.0
        if vv < 0.0:
            vv = 0.0
        if vv > 1000.0:
            vv = 1000.0
        out.append(vv)
    if not out:
        out = [1.0]
    return out
```
